golden-references 7 prompts

Generate these FIRST and settle them before anything else — they become the style for every asset that follows, and each one is attached as a reference image afterwards. They cite no reference of their own.

P001 char_body_male-01_se@2x.png char char/body
Subject: adult male torso and hips, standing, plain work shirt, facing south-east, arms and head omitted (they are separate rig parts)
Size: derived per part
Target file: char_body_male-01_se@2x.png
```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

[SIZE HINT: no taller than 144 px — one part of an assembled adult, drawn at the scale it will be assembled at]
P026 char_head_male-01_nw@2x.png char char/head
Subject: adult head and neck, neutral expression, no hair (hair is a separate part); variant male-01, facing nw
Size: derived per part
Target file: char_head_male-01_nw@2x.png
```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
```

Subject: hair only, sitting on an implied head, transparent where the head would be; variant long-01, facing ne
Size: derived per part
Target file: char_hair_long-01_ne@2x.png
```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.
```

Subject: road vehicle, empty, wheels visible, no driver; four-door commuter sedan in default, facing ne
Size: 410 x 301 px
Target file: veh_sedan_default_ne@2x.png
```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
```

```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
```

```
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

P114 struct_door_default@2x.png struct struct/door
Subject: plain service door in a frame
Size: 77 x 173 px
Target file: struct_door_default@2x.png
```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

[REFERENCE IMAGES: the approved golden reference set for this category]
---
[SUBJECT: plain service door in a frame]
[SIZE HINT: 77 x 173 px for a 1 x 0.2 x 2.1 m object]

P127 nature_tree_conifer-01_upper@2x.png nature nature/tree split half
Subject: narrow roadside conifer — the UPPER half only, cut at the same horizontal line as its partner
Size: 384 x 512 px
Target file: nature_tree_conifer-01_upper@2x.png
```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

[SUBJECT: interface icon drawn straight-on rather than in projection — flat, centred, single subject, generous margin, no background plate; upward chevron on a plinth]
[SIZE HINT: 128 x 128 px canvas]
P152 ui_icon_build@2x.png ui ui/icon
Subject: interface icon drawn straight-on rather than in projection — flat, centred, single subject, generous margin, no background plate; hammer and wrench crossed
Size: 128 x 128 px
Target file: ui_icon_build@2x.png
```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

[REFERENCE IMAGES: the approved golden reference set for this category]

```
ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

P165 ui_icon_unlock@2x.png ui ui/icon
Subject: interface icon drawn straight-on rather than in projection — flat, centred, single subject, generous margin, no background plate; open padlock
Size: 128 x 128 px
Target file: ui_icon_unlock@2x.png
```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT

are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete

No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
```

```
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

P198 veh_limo_default_ne@2x.png veh veh/limo new in this audit
Subject: stretched black VIP limousine, facing north-east (rear three-quarter), wheels visible, no driver
Size: derived per part

Status: MISSING + PROMPT ADDED
Runtime role: live traffic once art exists (baseShare currently 0)
Priority: P1 · Stage: 2-4 · Needed before: P20
```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.
```

Status: MISSING + PROMPT ADDED
Runtime role: road traffic receding from camera
Priority: P1 · Stage: all · Needed before: P20
```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

Subject: the delivered veh_van vehicle — SAME body, paint and wheel design as the existing set, facing north-east (rear three-quarter), wheels visible, no driver, BRAKE LIGHTS LIT — bright red tail lamps, subtle red glow on the body only
Size: derived per part
Target file: veh_van_brake_ne@2x.png
Status: MISSING + PROMPT ADDED
Runtime role: deceleration read (nose-dip today)
Priority: P2 · Stage: all · Needed before: P20
```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

[SIZE HINT: 64 x 64 px at 2x, transparent margin]

P273 struct_supervisor@2x.png ui ui/upgrade-icon new in this audit
Subject: upgrade card icon — supervisor, single readable object, slight isometric tilt matching the ui icon set
Size: derived per part
Target file: struct_supervisor@2x.png
Status: MISSING + PROMPT ADDED
Runtime role: premium upgrade cards (P18 consumes iconKey, unconsumed today)
Priority: P1 · Stage: per-rung · Needed before: P18
```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

```
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

P290 ui_icon_pause@2x.png ui ui/icon new in this audit

```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

P292 ui_icon_role-cook@2x.png ui ui/icon new in this audit
Subject: UI strip icon — role cook, single-glyph, instantly readable at 20 px
Size: derived per part
Target file: ui_icon_role-cook@2x.png
Status: MISSING + PROMPT ADDED
Runtime role: staff screen (P18)
Priority: P1 · Stage: 2-4 · Needed before: P18
```
STYLE
Clean, warm, lightly stylised isometric illustration.
NOT photorealistic. NOT pixel art. NOT heavy cartoon.
Reference feel: the upper segment of modern mobile tycoon games —
readable silhouettes, soft volume, calm colour.

CAMERA
2:1 dimetric isometric. Fixed. No exceptions.
Approximately 30 degrees elevation, 45 degrees yaw.
Orthographic — NO perspective convergence.

LIGHT
A single key light from the north-west (upper-left on screen), 35 degree pitch.
Shadow falls to the lower-right: soft, 30% opacity, cool and bluish.
Ambient is warm daylight. This NEVER changes — day/night is a runtime shader
tint, never baked into an asset.

OUTLINE
2px outer contour at 2x scale. Dark and colour-derived — the dark saturated
form of the object's own colour, NOT black.
NO interior line work. Volume is described by shading, not by lines.

DETAIL
Medium. Silhouette first. Must still be recognisable at 1x (half size).
Small details do not read at game size; they only add noise.

PALETTE
Locked 48-colour palette, attached. No colour outside it.
Twelve four-step ramps; step 900 is the shadow side and supplies the outline,
step 300 is the lit side.

BACKGROUND
Fully transparent RGBA. The ground shadow is a separate file, never baked in.

ANCHOR
Composed so the footprint centre — not the visual centre — sits on the ground
plane: between the feet for a character, the centre of the four wheels for a
vehicle, the centre of the base rectangle for furniture and buildings.

HEIGHT LIMIT
Nothing may exceed 160px tall at 2x scale as a single sprite. Taller subjects
are produced as two separate images, a lower and an upper part, each complete
and independently croppable.

TEXT
No text baked into the artwork. Signage lettering is decorative shape only.
```

Workflow: generate the golden references first and settle them · then one batch per session with the goldens attached · drop results in assets/source/ with their anchor sidecars · pnpm assets:validate · regenerate what fails, never lower a threshold · pnpm assets:build · pnpm assets:contact-sheet for the consistency review.
